diff --git a/engineering_agent/app/client_reporter.py b/engineering_agent/app/client_reporter.py
index 6696f898..e6825824 100644
--- a/engineering_agent/app/client_reporter.py
+++ b/engineering_agent/app/client_reporter.py
@@ -1,24 +1,153 @@
 """Client result reporter.
 
-Responsible for delivering cline-central results to the client after job completion.
-
-Current implementation: prints the result to stdout as JSON.
-Future implementation: call the client-facing SDK, which will POST the result to an
-API endpoint that persists it to HDLF — eliminating the need for clients to take on
-HDLF as a direct dependency.
+Responsible for delivering engineering-agent results to HDLF after job completion.
 """
+
 from __future__ import annotations
 
-import json
+from typing import Literal
+
+import httpx
+import structlog
+from pydantic import BaseModel
 
 from app.models import AgentTaskResult
 
+_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30.0
+_MAX_RESPONSE_PREVIEW = 500
+
+log = structlog.get_logger(__name__)
+
+
+class HdlfReportError(Exception):
+    """The hdlf-server rejected or could not process the result write."""
+
+
+def _preview(text: str) -> str:
+    if len(text) <= _MAX_RESPONSE_PREVIEW:
+        return text
+    return text[:_MAX_RESPONSE_PREVIEW] + "...(truncated)"
+
+
+class WriteHandlerResultRequest(BaseModel):
+    """Request body for PUT /api/v1/inspection/{inspection_id}/handler-results/{fault_id}.
+
+    Attributes:
+        result_json: Serialised AgentTaskResult payload. repo_diff is excluded —
+            it is sent separately as code_changes so hdlf-server persists it as
+            code_changes.diff rather than inside result.json.
+        output_description: Human-readable result summary. When None, the server
+            resolves it from the DB row created by the execution engine. Pass
+            explicitly to bypass DB resolution (e.g. in local testing).
+        scope: Handler scope. When None, resolved from the DB row.
+        code_changes: Unified diff of repository changes. None removes any
+            previously stored diff for this fault_id (strict-PUT semantics).
+    """
+
+    result_json: dict[str, object]
+    output_description: str | None = None
+    scope: Literal["pipeline_scoped", "stage_scoped"] | None = None
+    code_changes: str | None = None
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_agent_task_result(
+        cls,
+        result: AgentTaskResult,
+        *,
+        output_description: str | None = None,
+        scope: Literal["pipeline_scoped", "stage_scoped"] | None = None,
+    ) -> "WriteHandlerResultRequest":
+        """Build a WriteHandlerResultRequest from an AgentTaskResult.
+
+        repo_diff is mapped to code_changes and excluded from result_json to
+        avoid storing the diff twice.
+        """
+        return cls(
+            result_json=result.model_dump(exclude_none=True, exclude={"repo_diff"}),
+            output_description=output_description,
+            scope=scope,
+            code_changes=result.repo_diff,
+        )
 
-def report_result(result: AgentTaskResult) -> None:
-    """Deliver the cline-central result to the client.
 
-    Placeholder: prints the result JSON to stdout.
-    TODO: replace with client SDK call once the SDK and API endpoint are available.
-          The SDK will POST the result to an endpoint that saves it to HDLF.
+def report_result(
+    result: AgentTaskResult,
+    *,
+    inspection_id: str | None,
+    fault_id: str | None,
+    hdlf_base_url: str,
+    finalizer_task: bool = False,
+    output_description: str | None = None,
+    scope: Literal["pipeline_scoped", "stage_scoped"] | None = None,
+) -> None:
+    """Log the agent result and PUT it to hdlf-server unless this is a finalizer.
+
+    Args:
+        result: The completed agent task result.
+        inspection_id: UUID of the inspection this job belongs to.
+        fault_id: Logical handler identifier for this job (e.g. ``"engineering"``).
+        hdlf_base_url: Base URL of the in-cluster hdlf-server.
+        finalizer_task: When True, emit the completion result log but skip the
+            hdlf-server write. In this mode, inspection_id and fault_id may be
+            None.
+        output_description: Human-readable result summary. When None, the server
+            resolves it from the DB row created by the execution engine. Pass
+            explicitly to bypass DB resolution (e.g. in local testing).
+        scope: ``"pipeline_scoped"`` or ``"stage_scoped"``. When None, resolved
+            from the DB row. Pass explicitly to bypass DB resolution.
+
+    Raises:
+        HdlfReportError: The hdlf-server rejected or could not process the write,
+            or required handler-result IDs are missing for a non-finalizer task.
     """
-    print(json.dumps(result.model_dump(), indent=2), flush=True)
+    request = WriteHandlerResultRequest.from_agent_task_result(
+        result,
+        output_description=output_description,
+        scope=scope,
+    )
+
+    log.info(
+        "Completion result",
+        inspection_id=inspection_id,
+        body=request.model_dump_json(exclude_none=True),
+    )
+
+    if finalizer_task:
+        log.info(
+            "Finalizer task - skipping HDLF write",
+            inspection_id=inspection_id,
+            fault_id=fault_id,
+        )
+        return
+
+    if not inspection_id or not fault_id:
+        raise HdlfReportError(
+            "inspection_id and fault_id are required unless finalizer_task=True"
+        )
+
+    url = (
+        f"{hdlf_base_url.rstrip('/')}/api/v1/inspection/{inspection_id}/handler-results/{fault_id}"
+    )
+
+    log.info(
+        "Reporting result to hdlf-server",
+        inspection_id=inspection_id,
+        fault_id=fault_id,
+    )
+
+    try:
+        response = httpx.put(
+            url,
+            json=request.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
+            timeout=_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
+        )
+        response.raise_for_status()
+    except httpx.HTTPStatusError as exc:
+        raise HdlfReportError(
+            f"hdlf-server returned {exc.response.status_code} for {url}: "
+            f"{_preview(exc.response.text)}"
+        ) from exc
+    except httpx.RequestError as exc:
+        raise HdlfReportError(f"hdlf-server unreachable at {url}: {exc}") from exc
+
+    log.info("Result reported successfully", inspection_id=inspection_id, fault_id=fault_id)

+        hdlf_base_url: Base URL of the in-cluster hdlf-server. Defaults to the

     """
+
     task_id: str
+    inspection_id: str | None = None

     task_text: str

     config_repo_commitish: str
     mcps: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
     skills: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    hdlf_base_url: str = Field(default="http://pipeline-fl-control-plane-hdlf-server:8000")
+    finalizer_task: bool = False
 
     @field_validator("mcps", "skills", mode="before")
     @classmethod
@@ -44,3 +61,15 @@ def _parse_json_list(cls, v: str | list[str]) -> list[str]:
                 raise ValueError("must be a JSON array")
             return parsed
         return v
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _require_ids_for_non_finalizer(self) -> "JobSettings":
+        if not self.finalizer_task:
+            missing = []
+            if not self.inspection_id:
+                missing.append("INSPECTION_ID")
+            if not self.fault_id:
+                missing.append("FAULT_ID")
+            if missing:
+                raise ValueError(f"{', '.join(missing)} required when finalizer_task is False")

diff --git a/engineering_agent/scripts/run_agent_task.py b/engineering_agent/scripts/run_agent_task.py
index a10d5d0e..35403cc9 100644
--- a/engineering_agent/scripts/run_agent_task.py
+++ b/engineering_agent/scripts/run_agent_task.py
@@ -4,30 +4,46 @@
     Set env vars (via ConfigMap + Secret) and run:
         python scripts/run_agent_task.py
 
-    Result is printed as JSON to stdout. Exit code 0 = success, 1 = error.
+    Result is PUT to hdlf-server. Exit code 0 = success, 1 = error.
 """
+
 from __future__ import annotations
 
 import logging
 import sys
 
+import structlog
 from pydantic import ValidationError
 
-from app.client_reporter import report_result
+from app.client_reporter import HdlfReportError, report_result
 from app.config import JobSettings
 from app.models import AgentTaskRequest
 
 
-def main() -> None:
-    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, stream=sys.stderr,
-                        format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s")
+def _configure_logging() -> None:
+    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, stream=sys.stderr, format="%(message)s", force=True)
+    structlog.configure(
+        processors=[
+            structlog.contextvars.merge_contextvars,
+            structlog.processors.add_log_level,
+            structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt="iso", utc=True),
+            structlog.processors.ExceptionRenderer(),
+            structlog.processors.JSONRenderer(),
+        ],
+        wrapper_class=structlog.make_filtering_bound_logger(logging.INFO),
+        logger_factory=structlog.WriteLoggerFactory(file=sys.stderr),
+        cache_logger_on_first_use=False,
+    )
 
-    log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+def main() -> None:
+    _configure_logging()
+    log = structlog.get_logger(__name__)
 
     try:
         settings = JobSettings()
     except ValidationError as exc:
-        log.error("Error loading environment configuration: %s", exc)
+        log.error("Error loading environment configuration", errors=exc.errors())
         sys.exit(1)
 
     # Deferred import — logging must be configured before agent_task_runner loads,
@@ -46,16 +62,26 @@ def main() -> None:
             skills=settings.skills,
         )
     except ValidationError as exc:
-        log.error("Error building agent task request: %s", exc)
+        log.error("Error building agent task request", errors=exc.errors())
         sys.exit(1)
 
     try:
-        result = agent_task_runner.run(request)
+        result = agent_task_runner.run(request, inspection_id=settings.inspection_id)
     except Exception:
         log.error("Unhandled exception in agent task runner", exc_info=True)
         sys.exit(1)
 
-    report_result(result)
+    try:
+        report_result(
+            result,
+            inspection_id=settings.inspection_id,
+            fault_id=settings.fault_id,
+            hdlf_base_url=settings.hdlf_base_url,
+            finalizer_task=settings.finalizer_task,
+        )
+    except HdlfReportError:
+        log.error("Failed to report result to hdlf-server", exc_info=True)
+        sys.exit(1)
 
 
 if __name__ == "__main__":
diff --git a/engineering_agent/tests/test_agent_task_runner.py b/engineering_agent/tests/test_agent_task_runner.py
index d3f03c76..0b674ddc 100644
--- a/engineering_agent/tests/test_agent_task_runner.py
+++ b/engineering_agent/tests/test_agent_task_runner.py
@@ -49,7 +49,7 @@ def test_run_success():
 
     with patch("app.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_p3), \
          patch("app.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_sd):
-        result = run(req)
+        result = run(req, inspection_id="test-inspection-1")
 
     assert result.task_id == req.task_id
     assert result.completion_text == "all done"
@@ -75,9 +75,7 @@ def test_run_sdk_exception_during_collect():
     with patch("app.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_p3), \
          patch("app.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_sd):
         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="collect failed"):
-            run(req)
-
-
+            run(req, inspection_id="test-inspection-1")
 def test_run_completion_text_failure_raises():
     """If get_completion_text raises, the exception propagates out of run()."""
     req = make_request()
@@ -87,4 +85,4 @@ def test_run_completion_text_failure_raises():
     with patch("app.agent_task_runner.pipeline3", mock_p3), \
          patch("app.agent_task_runner.scheduling_doit", mock_sd):
         with pytest.raises(KeyError):
-            run(req)

diff --git a/engineering_agent/tests/test_client_reporter.py b/engineering_agent/tests/test_client_reporter.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..d5dff525
--- /dev/null
+++ b/engineering_agent/tests/test_client_reporter.py
@@ -0,0 +1,225 @@
+"""Unit tests for app.client_reporter."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import httpx
+import pytest
+
+from app.client_reporter import HdlfReportError, WriteHandlerResultRequest, report_result
+from app.models import AgentTaskResult
+
+
+def _make_result(**overrides: object) -> AgentTaskResult:
+    defaults = dict(
+        task_id="test-task-001",
+        completion_text="Fixed the bug",
+        repo_diff=None,
+        tokens_in=100,
+        tokens_out=50,
+        mlflow_run_id="mlflow-run-abc",
+    )
+    return AgentTaskResult(**{**defaults, **overrides})
+
+
+def _call_report(result: AgentTaskResult, **overrides: object) -> None:
+    kwargs = dict(
+        inspection_id="bb08c8cc-7387-45e9-88fc-c112dca6a934",
+        fault_id="engineering",
+        hdlf_base_url="http://hdlf.test:8000",
+    )
+    kwargs.update(overrides)
+    report_result(result, **kwargs)
+
+
+def _ok_response() -> httpx.Response:
+    return httpx.Response(204, request=httpx.Request("PUT", "http://hdlf.test"))
+
+
+def _error_response(status_code: int, text: str = "") -> httpx.Response:
+    return httpx.Response(status_code, text=text, request=httpx.Request("PUT", "http://hdlf.test"))
+
+
+# ── WriteHandlerResultRequest.from_agent_task_result ────────────────────────
+
+
+def test_from_agent_task_result_no_diff():
+    """repo_diff=None → code_changes absent, repo_diff excluded from result_json."""
+    result = _make_result()
+    req = WriteHandlerResultRequest.from_agent_task_result(result)
+    assert req.code_changes is None
+    assert "repo_diff" not in req.result_json
+
+
+def test_from_agent_task_result_with_diff():
+    """repo_diff set → code_changes populated, repo_diff excluded from result_json."""
+    result = _make_result(repo_diff="diff --git a/f b/f\n+fix")
+    req = WriteHandlerResultRequest.from_agent_task_result(result)
+    assert req.code_changes == "diff --git a/f b/f\n+fix"
+    assert "repo_diff" not in req.result_json
+
+
+def test_from_agent_task_result_output_description_and_scope():
+    """output_description and scope are forwarded onto the model."""
+    result = _make_result()
+    req = WriteHandlerResultRequest.from_agent_task_result(
+        result,
+        output_description="Agent fixed lint issues",
+        scope="pipeline_scoped",
+    )
+    assert req.output_description == "Agent fixed lint issues"
+    assert req.scope == "pipeline_scoped"
+
+
+# ── report_result ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+
+def test_success_204_no_diff():
+    """204 response with no repo_diff — result_json sent, no code_changes in body."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result)
+
+    mock_put.assert_called_once()
+    body = mock_put.call_args.kwargs["json"]
+    assert body["result_json"]["task_id"] == "test-task-001"
+    assert "code_changes" not in body
+    assert "repo_diff" not in body["result_json"]
+
+
+def test_success_204_with_diff():
+    """204 response with repo_diff — diff sent as code_changes, not inside result_json."""
+    result = _make_result(repo_diff="diff --git a/f b/f\n+fix")
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result)
+
+    body = mock_put.call_args.kwargs["json"]
+    assert body["code_changes"] == "diff --git a/f b/f\n+fix"
+    assert "repo_diff" not in body["result_json"]
+
+
+def test_output_description_and_scope_included_when_provided():
+    """output_description and scope are included in the body when explicitly passed."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result, output_description="Agent fixed lint issues", scope="pipeline_scoped")
+
+    body = mock_put.call_args.kwargs["json"]
+    assert body["output_description"] == "Agent fixed lint issues"
+    assert body["scope"] == "pipeline_scoped"
+
+
+def test_output_description_and_scope_omitted_when_none():
+    """output_description and scope are absent from body when None — triggers DB resolution."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result)
+
+    body = mock_put.call_args.kwargs["json"]
+    assert "output_description" not in body
+    assert "scope" not in body
+
+
+def test_finalizer_task_logs_completion_and_skips_hdlf_write():
+    """Finalizer tasks log the completion payload but do not write to hdlf-server."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put") as mock_put, patch(
+        "app.client_reporter.log.info"
+    ) as mock_info:
+        report_result(
+            result,
+            inspection_id=None,
+            fault_id=None,
+            hdlf_base_url="http://hdlf.test:8000",
+            finalizer_task=True,
+        )
+
+    mock_put.assert_not_called()
+    assert any(
+        call.args[0] == "Completion result"
+        for call in mock_info.call_args_list
+    )
+    assert any(
+        call.args[0] == "Finalizer task - skipping HDLF write"
+        for call in mock_info.call_args_list
+    )
+
+
+def test_non_finalizer_requires_handler_result_ids():
+    """Non-finalizer tasks fail clearly before attempting an hdlf-server write."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put") as mock_put:
+        with pytest.raises(HdlfReportError, match="inspection_id and fault_id"):
+            report_result(
+                result,
+                inspection_id=None,
+                fault_id="engineering",
+                hdlf_base_url="http://hdlf.test:8000",
+            )
+
+    mock_put.assert_not_called()
+
+
+def test_url_constructed_correctly():
+    """PUT is sent to the correct hdlf-server URL."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result)
+
+    url = mock_put.call_args.args[0]
+    assert (
+        url
+        == "http://hdlf.test:8000/api/v1/inspection/bb08c8cc-7387-45e9-88fc-c112dca6a934/handler-results/engineering"
+    )
+
+
+def test_trailing_slash_in_base_url_stripped():
+    """Trailing slash in hdlf_base_url does not produce a double-slash URL."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_ok_response()) as mock_put:
+        _call_report(result, hdlf_base_url="http://hdlf.test:8000/")
+
+    url = mock_put.call_args.args[0]
+    assert "//" not in url.replace("http://", "")
+
+
+def test_network_error_raises_hdlf_report_error():
+    """httpx.RequestError is wrapped in HdlfReportError."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", side_effect=httpx.ConnectError("refused")):
+        with pytest.raises(HdlfReportError, match="hdlf-server unreachable"):
+            _call_report(result)
+
+
+def test_non_204_status_raises_hdlf_report_error():
+    """Any 4xx/5xx response raises HdlfReportError with status code in message."""
+    result = _make_result()
+
+    with patch(
+        "app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_error_response(500, "internal error")
+    ):
+        with pytest.raises(HdlfReportError, match="500"):
+            _call_report(result)
+
+
+def test_response_body_truncated_in_error_message():
+    """A large error response body is truncated to 500 chars in the HdlfReportError message."""
+    result = _make_result()
+    large_body = "x" * 1000
+
+    with patch("app.client_reporter.httpx.put", return_value=_error_response(502, large_body)):
+        with pytest.raises(HdlfReportError) as exc_info:
+            _call_report(result)
+
+    assert "...(truncated)" in str(exc_info.value)
+    assert "x" * 501 not in str(exc_info.value)

+
+[[package]]
+name = "aiosignal"
+version = "1.4.0"
+source = { registry = "https://int.repositories.cloud.sap/artifactory/api/pypi/build-releases-pypi/simple" }
+dependencies = [
+    { name = "frozenlist" },
+    { name = "typing-extensions" },
+]
+sdist = { url = "https://int.repositories.cloud.sap/artifactory/api/pypi/build-releases-pypi/packages/packages/61/62/06741b579156360248d1ec624842ad0edf697050bbaf7c3e46394e106ad1/aiosignal-1.4.0.tar.gz", hash = "sha256:f47eecd9468083c2029cc99945502cb7708b082c232f9aca65da147157b251c7" }
+wheels = [
+    { url = "https://int.repositories.cloud.sap/artifactory/api/pypi/build-releases-pypi/packages/packages/fb/76/641ae371508676492379f16e2fa48f4e2c11741bd63c48be4b12a6b09cba/aiosignal-1.4.0-py3-none-any.whl", hash = "sha256:053243f8b92b990551949e63930a839ff0cf0b0ebbe0597b0f3fb19e1a0fe82e" },
+]
+
+[[package]]
